ProjectStorageCreateResultSchema,\n  type ProjectStorageOpenRequest,\n  type ProjectStorageOpenResult,\n  ProjectStorageOpenResultSchema,\n} from \"@slopstop/protocol\";\nimport { Effect, Match, type desktopText = {\n  \"invalid-command\": harnessSendFailureMessage({\n    ok: false,\n    error: { code: \"HARNESS_SESSION_MESSAGE_INVALID\" },\n  }),\n  ...bridgeNeutralText.desktop,\n  \"mismatched-event\": \"Harness returned a mismatched Project Storage response.\",\n  \"mismatched-echo\": \"Harness returned a mismatched Project Storage request.\",\n} as const;\n\nconst response.\",\n} as const;\n\n/** The fixed text a Project Storage request answers for each fault. */\nfunction faultText(fault: ProjectRequestFault): string {\n  return Match.value(fault).pipe(\n    Match.discriminatorsExhaustive(\"kind\")({\n      transport: () => bridgeNeutralText.transport,\n      protocol: ({ reason }) => protocolText[reason],\n      harness: ({ message }) => message,\n      send: ({ code }) => harnessSendFailureMessage({ ok: false, error: { code } }),\n      unsent: () => stoppedText,\n      stopped: () => stoppedText,\n      desktop: ({ reason }) => desktopText[reason],\n    }),\n  );\n}\n\nfunction brokenResult<Request, Result>(\n  schema: Schema.Decoder<Result>,\n  request: Request,\n  message: string,\n): Result {\n  return decodeStrict(schema, {\n    status: \"broken\",\n    request,\n    diagnostic: { code: \"PROJECT_STORAGE_TRANSPORT_FAILED\", message },\n  });\n}\n\nexport interface ProjectStorageBridgeClient {\n  open(request: ProjectStorageOpenRequest): Promise<ProjectStorageOpenResult>;\n  create(request: ProjectStorageCreateRequest): Promise<ProjectStorageCreateResult>;\n  close(request: ProjectStorageCloseRequest): Promise<ProjectStorageCloseResult>;\n  stop(): void;\n}\n\n/**\n * The Project Storage bridge over a request core. `afterStop` runs once, inside the bridge's own\n * stop: the standalone bridge ends its private core there; a shared one leaves the core alone.\n */\nexport function projectStorageBridgeOver(\n  core: HarnessRequestCore,\n  clock: Clock,\n  afterStop: () => void,\n): ProjectStorageBridgeClient {\n  let stopped = false;\n  const metadata = () => ({ messageId: clock.createId(), sentAt: clock.now() });\n  function operation<Request, Result extends Readonly<{ request: Request }>>(\n    request: Request,\n    resultSchema: Schema.Decoder<Result>,\n    createCommand: (request: Request) => DesktopMessage,\n    event: HarnessMessage[\"event\"],\n  ): Promise<Result> {\n    const broken = (fault: ProjectRequestFault) =>\n      brokenResult(resultSchema, request, faultText(fault));\n    if (stopped) return Promise.resolve(broken({ kind: \"unsent\" }));\n    // An invalid request fails in the command builder, as `desktop` `invalid-command`. The echo\n    // is checked against the command's validated payload, never the caller's object.\n    let command: DesktopMessage | undefined;\n    const build = () => {\n      command = createCommand(request);\n      return command;\n    };\n    return answerRequest(\n      core.request(\"project-storage\", build, event).pipe(\n        Effect.flatMap((value) => decodeReply(resultSchema, value)),\n        Effect.flatMap((result) =>\n          isDeepStrictEqual(result.request, command?.payload)\n            ? {\n  type DesktopMessage,\n  decodeStrictResult,\n  type HarnessFailureCode,\n  type HarnessMessage,\n} from \"@slopstop/protocol\";\nimport { Deferred, Effect, Result, type Schema } from \"effect\";\nimport type { HarnessSessionClient, HarnessSessionSendResult } from \"./harness-session.js\";\n\ntype SendErrorCode = Extract<HarnessSessionSendResult, { ok: false }>[\"error\"][\"code\"];\n\n/**\n * Why a harness request ended without a payload. It is a value the bridges map into their\n * envelopes, never thrown.\n */\nexport type ProjectRequestFault =\n  | Readonly<{ kind: \"transport\" }>\n  | Readonly<{\n      kind: \"protocol\";\n      reason: \"invalid-message\" | \"uncorrelated\" | \"system-failure\" | \"undecodable\";\n    }>\n  | Readonly<{ kind: \"harness\"; code: HarnessFailureCode; message: string }>\n  | Readonly<{ kind: \"send\"; code: SendErrorCode }>\n  | Readonly<{ kind: \"unsent\" }>\n  | Readonly<{ kind: \"stopped\" }>\n  | Readonly<{\n      kind: \"desktop\";\n      reason: \"invalid-command\" | \"identity-collision\" | \"mismatched-event\" | \"mismatched-echo\";\n    }>;\n\n/** The `{ createId, now }` clock a bridge stamps its commands with. */\nexport type Clock = Readonly<{ createId(): string; now(): string }>;\n\n/**\n * The fault texts that name no bridge, shared by the Project Storage and workspace bridges. The\n * bridge-specific texts (uncorrelated, undecodable, mismatched, stopped) stay with each bridge.\n */\nexport const bridgeNeutralText = {\n  transport: \"Harness session disconnected.\",\n  protocol: {\n    \"invalid-message\": \"Harness session received an invalid protocol message.\",\n    \"system-failure\": \"Harness reported a failure.\",\n  },\n  desktop: { \"identity-collision\": \"Harness request identity collided.\" },\n} as const;\n\n/** The bridge a request belongs to, so a bridge stop settles only its own requests. */\ntype HarnessBridgeTag = \"project-entry\" | \"project-storage\" | \"workspace\";\n\ntype Entry = Readonly<{\n  bridge: HarnessBridgeTag;\n  expected: HarnessMessage[\"event\"];\n  done: Deferred.Deferred<unknown, ProjectRequestFault>;\n}>;\n\nexport interface HarnessRequestCore {\n  /** Sends one command and waits for its correlated reply payload. */\n  request(\n    bridge: HarnessBridgeTag,\n    createCommand: () => DesktopMessage,\n    expected: HarnessMessage[\"event\"],\n  ): Effect.Effect<unknown, ProjectRequestFault>;\n  /** Settles every pending request of one bridge with `fault`. */\n  failBridge(bridge: HarnessBridgeTag, fault: ProjectRequestFault): void;\n  /** Settles every pending request with `fault`. */\n  failAll(fault: ProjectRequestFault): void;\n  /** Observes the session's non-reply messages; returns the unsubscribe. */\n  observe(listener: (message: HarnessMessage) => void): () => void;\n  /** Ends the core's one session subscription. */\n  unsubscribe(): void;\n}\n\n/** Only `*.result` events and `request.failure` answer a request. */\nfunction isReply(event: HarnessMessage[\"event\"]): boolean {\n  return event === \"request.failure\" || event.endsWith(\".result\");\n}\n\nexport function makeHarnessRequestCore(session: HarnessSessionClient): HarnessRequestCore {\n  const entries = new Map<string, Entry>();\n  const observers = new Set<(message: HarnessMessage) => void>();\n  const settle = (id: string, completion: Effect.Effect<unknown, ProjectRequestFault>) => {\n    const entry = entries.get(id);\n    if (entry === undefined) return;\n    entries.delete(id);\n    Deferred.doneUnsafe(entry.done, completion);\n  };\n  const failWhere = (match: (entry: Entry) => boolean, fault: ProjectRequestFault) => {\n    for (const [id, entry] of [...entries]) if (match(entry)) settle(id, Effect.fail(fault));\n  };\n  const failAll = (fault: ProjectRequestFault) => failWhere(() => true, fault);\n  const receive = (message: HarnessMessage) => {\n    if (message.event === \"system.failure\") {\n      failAll({ kind: \"protocol\", reason: \"system-failure\" });\n      return;\n    }\n    if (!isReply(message.event)) {\n      for (const observer of [...observers]) observer(message);\n      return;\n    }\n    if (message.causationId === null) {\n      failAll({ kind: \"protocol\", reason: \"uncorrelated\" });\n      return;\n    }\n    if (message.event === \"request.failure\") {\n      const { code, message: text } = message.payload;\n      settle(message.causationId, Effect.fail({ kind: \"harness\", code, message: text }));\n      return;\n    }\n    const entry = entries.get(message.causationId);\n    if (entry === undefined) return;\n    settle(\n      message.causationId,\n      entry.expected === message.event\n        ? Effect.succeed(message.payload)\n        : Effect.fail({ kind: \"desktop\", reason: \"mismatched-event\" }),\n    );\n  };\n  // A throwing send is refused like a failed post: the request settles and frees its id.\n  const sendGuarded = (command: DesktopMessage): HarnessSessionSendResult => {\n    try {\n      return session.send(command);\n    } catch {\n      return { ok: false, error: { code: \"HARNESS_SESSION_SEND_FAILED\" } };\n    }\n  };\n  const unsubscribe = session.subscribe((event) => {\n    if (event.type === \"disconnected\") failAll({ kind: \"transport\" });\n    else if (event.type === \"protocol-error\")\n      failAll({ kind: \"protocol\", reason: \"invalid-message\" });\n    else receive(event.message);\n  });\n  return {\n    request: (bridge, createCommand, expected) =>\n      Effect.suspend(() => {\n        let command: DesktopMessage;\n        try {\n          command = createCommand();\n        } catch {\n          return Effect.fail({ kind: \"desktop\", reason: \"invalid-command\" } as const);\n        }\n        const id = command.messageId;\n        if (entries.has(id))\n          return Effect.fail({ kind: \"desktop\", reason: \"identity-collision\" } as const);\n        const done = Deferred.makeUnsafe<unknown, ProjectRequestFault>();\n        entries.set(id, { bridge, expected, done });\n        const sent = sendGuarded(command);\n        if (!sent.ok) settle(id, Effect.fail({ kind: \"send\", code: sent.error.code }));\n        // An interrupted request frees its id, so the same id can be sent again.\n        return Effect.onInterrupt(Deferred.await(done), () =>\n          Effect.sync(() => {\n            if (entries.get(id)?.done === done) entries.delete(id);\n          }),\n        );\n      }),\n    failBridge: (bridge, fault) => failWhere((entry) => entry.bridge === bridge, fault),\n    failAll,\n    observe: (observer) => {\n      observers.add(observer);\n      return () => {\n        observers.delete(observer);\n      };\n    },\n    unsubscribe,\n  };\n}\n\n/** Decodes a reply payload; a payload that does not decode is a `protocol` `undecodable` fault. */\nexport function decodeReply<A>(\n  schema: Schema.Decoder<A>,\n  value: unknown,\n): Effect.Effect<A, ProjectRequestFault> {\n  const decoded = decodeStrictResult(schema, value);\n  return Result.isSuccess(decoded)\n    ? Effect.succeed(decoded.success)\n    : Effect.fail({ kind: \"protocol\", reason: \"undecodable\" });\n}\n\n/**\n * Runs a bridge request to its envelope: a fault becomes `onFault`'s answer, so the promise never\n * rejects for an expected failure.\n *\n * Temporary Promise facade (P-S7-1 (b)): remove when C2 streaming moves the IPC edge and the\n * package-smoke callers onto the runtime.\n */\nexport function answerRequest<A>(\n  request: Effect.Effect<A, ProjectRequestFault>,\n  onFault: (fault: ProjectRequestFault) => A,\n): Promise<A> {\n  return Effect.runPromise(request.pipe(Effect.catch((fault) => Effect.succeed(onFault(fault)))));\n}\n"},"apps/desktop/src/main/harness-session.ts":{"language":"typescript","mutants":[{"id":"162","mutatorName":"BlockStatement","replacement":"{}","statusReason":"expected {\n  type HarnessFailureCode,\n  type ProjectConversationReadRequest,\n  type ProjectConversationReadResult,\n  type ProjectConversationSaveRequest,\n  type ProjectConversationSaveResult,\n  projectConversationDiagnostics,\n} from \"@slopstop/protocol\";\nimport { Match } from \"effect\";\nimport type { ProjectRequestFault } from \"./harness-request-core.js\";\n\nconst rows = projectConversationDiagnostics;\nconst harnessRows = {\n  HARNESS_INTERNAL_FAILURE: rows.harnessInternalFailure,\n  PROTOCOL_MESSAGE_INVALID: rows.protocolMessageInvalid,\n  PROTOCOL_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED: rows.protocolVersionUnsupported,\n} as const;\n\n/** What a fault means for a conversation request, before the operation decides its result. */\ntype FaultMeaning =\n  | Readonly<{ meaning: \"not-sent\" }>\n  | Readonly<{ meaning: \"sent-then-lost\" }>\n  | Readonly<{ meaning: \"malformed-reply\" }>\n  | Readonly<{ meaning: \"defect\" }>\n  | Readonly<{ meaning: \"harness\"; code: HarnessFailureCode }>;\n\n/**\n * The shared fault table (G25). Not sent: a call after stop or a refused send. Sent, then lost: a\n * disconnect or a stop while pending. A malformed reply is a `protocol` fault, whatever its\n * reason: `invalid-message`, `uncorrelated`, `system-failure` or `undecodable`. A decodable wrong\n * reply, an unbuildable command, a colliding id or a send refused as an invalid message points to\n * a defect.\n */\nfunction faultMeaning(fault: ProjectRequestFault): FaultMeaning {\n  return Match.value(fault).pipe(\n    Match.discriminatorsExhaustive(\"kind\")({\n      unsent: (): FaultMeaning => ({ meaning: \"not-sent\" }),\n      transport: (): FaultMeaning => ({ meaning: \"sent-then-lost\" }),\n      stopped: (): FaultMeaning => ({ meaning: \"sent-then-lost\" }),\n      protocol: (): FaultMeaning => ({ meaning: \"malformed-reply\" }),\n      desktop: (): FaultMeaning => ({ meaning: \"defect\" }),\n      harness: ({ code }): FaultMeaning => ({ meaning: \"harness\", code }),\n      send: ({ code }): FaultMeaning =>\n        code === \"HARNESS_SESSION_MESSAGE_INVALID\"\n          ? { meaning: \"defect\" }\n          : { meaning: \"not-sent\" },\n    }),\n  );\n}\n\n/** A read that got no decoded reply: a lost read is `unavailable`; a malformed reply is `broken`. */\nexport function readConversationFailure(\n  request: ProjectConversationReadRequest,\n  fault: ProjectRequestFault,\n): ProjectConversationReadResult {\n  const meaning = faultMeaning(fault);\n  if (meaning.meaning === \"harness\")\n    return { status: \"broken\", request, diagnostic: harnessRows[meaning.code] };\n  if (meaning.meaning === \"not-sent\" || meaning.meaning === \"sent-then-lost\")\n    return { status: \"unavailable\", request, diagnostic: rows.connectionLost };\n  return { status: \"broken\", request, diagnostic: rows.protocolMessageInvalid };\n}\n\n/**\n * A save that got no decoded reply. Once sent, the harness may have committed it, so a lost\n * connection and a malformed reply are `uncertain`; the kept `saveId` lets a later press replay\n * it (G25, G27). A save never sent is `unavailable`.\n */\nexport function saveConversationFailure(\n  request: ProjectConversationSaveRequest,\n  fault: ProjectRequestFault,\n): ProjectConversationSaveResult {\n  const meaning = faultMeaning(fault);\n  if (meaning.meaning === \"harness\")\n    return { status: \"broken\", request, diagnostic: harnessRows[meaning.code] };\n  if (meaning.meaning === \"not-sent\")\n    return { status: \"unavailable\", request, diagnostic: rows.connectionLost };\n  if (meaning.meaning === \"defect\")\n    return { status: \"broken\", request, diagnostic: rows.protocolMessageInvalid };\n  return { status: \"uncertain\", request, diagnostic: rows.saveUnconfirmed };\n}\n"},"apps/desktop/src/main/project-entry-bridge.ts":{"language":"typescript","mutants":[{"id":"219","mutatorName":"BlockStatement","replacement":"{}","statusReason":"Cannot destructure property fault.\nfunction unavailable<Request, Result>(\n  resultSchema: Schema.Decoder<Result>,\n  status: string,\n  retryable: boolean,\n) {\n  return (request: Request): Result =>\n    decodeStrict(resultSchema, {\n      status,\n      request,\n      diagnostic: {\n        code: \"PROJECT_COORDINATOR_UNAVAILABLE\",\n        message: projectAvailabilityMessages.connectionLost,\n        retryable,\n      },\n    });\n}\n\n/** The activation result while the Project coordinator is unreachable. */\nexport const projectActivationUnavailable = unavailable<\n  CanonicalProjectActivationRequest,\n  CanonicalProjectActivationResult\n>(CanonicalProjectActivationResultSchema, \"unavailable\", true);\n\n/** The switch result while the Project coordinator is \"coordinator-unavailable\", false);\n\n/** The fault of a request made after the bridge stopped: it was never sent. */\nexport const unsent = { kind: \"unsent\" } as const;\n\n/** The list answer while SlopStop closes (D11): at the IPC gate and for work in flight at stop. */\nexport const projectListClosing = {\n  status: \"unavailable\",\n  code: \"PROJECT_LIST_UNAVAILABLE\",\n} as const;\n\n/** The request helpers of one project-entry bridge, over its core and its stopped flag. */\nfunction entryRequests(core: HarnessRequestCore, clock: Clock, isStopped: () => boolean) {\n  const metadata = () => ({ messageId: clock.createId(), sentAt: clock.now() });\n  function request<Result>(\n    createCommand: () => DesktopMessage,\n    event: HarnessMessage[\"event\"],\n    decode: (value: unknown) => Effect.Effect<Result, ProjectRequestFault>,\n    broken: (fault: ProjectRequestFault) => Result,\n  ): Promise<Result> {\n    if (isStopped()) return Promise.resolve(broken(unsent));\n    return answerRequest(\n      core.request(\"project-entry\", createCommand, event).pipe(Effect.flatMap(decode)),\n      broken,\n    );\n  }\n  // These results echo their request: a mismatched echo is a `desktop` fault.\n  function echoing<Request, Result extends Readonly<{ request: Request }>>(\n    operation: Readonly<{\n      requestSchema: Schema.Decoder<Request>;\n      resultSchema: Schema.Decoder<Result>;\n      command(\n        metadata: Readonly<{ messageId: string; sentAt: string }>,\n        request: Request,\n      ): DesktopMessage;\n      event: HarnessMessage[\"event\"];\n      broken(request: Request, fault: ProjectRequestFault): Result;\n    }>,\n  ) {\n    return (input: Request): Promise<Result> => {\n      const inputRequest = decodeStrict(operation.requestSchema, input);\n      return request(\n        () => operation.command(metadata(), inputRequest),\n        operation.event,\n        (value) =>\n          Effect.flatMap(decodeReply(operation.resultSchema, value), (result) =>\n            isDeepStrictEqual(result.request, inputRequest)\n              ? Effect.succeed(result)\n              : Effect.fail({ kind: \"desktop\", reason: \"mismatched-echo\" } as const),\n          ),\n        (fault) => operation.broken(inputRequest, fault),\n      );\n    };\n  }\n  return { metadata, request, echoing };\n}\n\n/** The project-entry operations over one bridge's request helpers. */\nfunction entryOperations({ metadata, request, echoing }: ReturnType<typeof entryRequests>) {\n  return {\n    list: () =>\n      request(\n        () => createProjectListCommand(metadata()),\n        \"project.list.result\",\n        (value) => decodeReply(ProjectListResultSchema, value),\n        (fault) =>\n          fault.kind === \"stopped\"\n            ? projectListClosing\n            : { status: \"broken\" as const, code: \"PROJECT_LIST_TRANSPORT_FAILED\" as const },\n      ),\n    register: (input: ProjectRegistrationRequest) => {\n      const step = decodeStrict(ProjectRegistrationRequestSchema, input);\n      return request(\n        () => createProjectRegistrationCommand(metadata(), step),\n        \"project.registration.result\",\n        (value) => decodeReply(ProjectRegistrationResultSchema, value),\n        (fault) =>\n          fault.kind === \"stopped\"\n            ? projectRegistrationClosing\n            : {\n                status: \"broken\" as const,\n                code: \"PROJECT_REGISTRATION_TRANSPORT_FAILED\" as const,\n              },\n      );\n    },\n    activate: echoing({\n      requestSchema: CanonicalProjectActivationRequestSchema,\n      resultSchema: CanonicalProjectActivationResultSchema,\n      command: createProjectActivateCommand,\n      event: \"project.activate.result\",\n      broken: projectActivationUnavailable,\n    }),\n    switchProject: echoing({\n      requestSchema: CanonicalProjectSwitchRequestSchema,\n      resultSchema: CanonicalProjectSwitchResultSchema,\n      command: createProjectSwitchCommand,\n      event: \"project.switch.result\",\n      broken: projectSwitchUnavailable,\n    }),\n    upgrade: echoing({\n      requestSchema: ProjectUpgradeRequestSchema,\n      resultSchema: ProjectUpgradeResultSchema,\n      command: createProjectUpgradeCommand,\n      event: \"project.upgrade.result\",\n      broken: projectUpgradeFailure,\n    }),\n    readConversation: echoing({\n      requestSchema: ProjectConversationReadRequestSchema,\n      resultSchema: ProjectConversationReadResultSchema,\n      command: createProjectConversationReadCommand,\n      event: \"project.conversation.read.result\",\n      broken: readConversationFailure,\n    }),\n    saveConversationMessage: echoing({\n      requestSchema: ProjectConversationSaveRequestSchema,\n      resultSchema: ProjectConversationSaveResultSchema,\n      command: createProjectConversationSaveCommand,\n      event: \"project.conversation.save.result\",\n      broken: saveConversationFailure,\n    }),\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * The project-entry bridge over a request core. `afterStop` runs once, after the bridge's own\n * stop: the standalone bridge ends its private core there; a shared one leaves the core alone.\n */\nexport function projectEntryBridgeOver(\n  core: HarnessRequestCore,\n  clock: Clock,\n  afterStop: () => void,\n) {\n  let stopped = false;\n  return {\n    ...entryOperations(entryRequests(core, clock, () => stopped)),\n    stop: () => {\n      if (stopped) return;\n      stopped = true;\n      // Settle first: an unsubscribe failure in `afterStop` surfaces only after.\n      core.failBridge(\"project-entry\", { kind: \"stopped\" });\n      afterStop();\n    },\n  };\n}\nexport type ProjectEntryBridge = ReturnType<typeof projectEntryBridgeOver>;\n\n/** A standalone project-entry bridge, with its own core over `session`. */\nexport function createProjectEntryBridge(\n  options: Readonly<{ session: HarnessSessionClient } & Clock>,\n): ProjectEntryBridge {\n  const core = makeHarnessRequestCore(options.session);\n  return projectEntryBridgeOver(core, options, {\n  type ProjectUpgradeRequest,\n  type ProjectUpgradeResult,\n  projectUpgradeDiagnostics,\n} from \"@slopstop/protocol\";\nimport { Match } from \"effect\";\nimport type { ProjectRequestFault } from \"./harness-request-core.js\";\n\nconst rows = projectUpgradeDiagnostics;\nconst harnessRows = {\n  HARNESS_INTERNAL_FAILURE: rows.harnessInternalFailure,\n  PROTOCOL_MESSAGE_INVALID: rows.protocolMessageInvalid,\n  PROTOCOL_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED: rows.protocolVersionUnsupported,\n} as const;\n\nconst connectionLost = (request: ProjectUpgradeRequest): ProjectUpgradeResult => ({\n Only a lost or stopped\n * connection is `unavailable`; a harness failure keeps its code, and every other fault is an\n * invalid protocol message.\n */\nexport function projectUpgradeFailure(\n  request: ProjectUpgradeRequest,\n  fault: ProjectRequestFault,\n): ProjectUpgradeResult {\n  return Match.value(fault).pipe(\n    Match.discriminatorsExhaustive(\"kind\")({\n      transport: () => connectionLost(request),\n      unsent: () => connectionLost(request),\n      stopped: () => connectionLost(request),\n      protocol: () => protocolInvalid(request),\n      desktop: () => protocolInvalid(request),\n      harness: ({ code }): ProjectUpgradeResult => ({\n        status: \"broken\",\n        request,\n        diagnostic: harnessRows[code],\n      }),\n      send: ({ code type WorkspaceQueryResult,\n  WorkspaceQueryResultSchema,\n  type WorkspaceScope,\n} from \"@slopstop/protocol\";\nimport { Effect, Match, type Schema } from \"effect\";\nimport {\n  answerRequest,\n  bridgeNeutralText,\n  type Clock,\n  decodeReply,\n  type HarnessRequestCore,\n  makeHarnessRequestCore,\n  type ProjectRequestFault,\n} from \"./harness-request-core.js\";\nimport { HarnessSessionClient;\n  createId: () => string;\n  now: () => string;\n}>;\n\n/** The capability an intent belongs to. */\nexport function capabilityForIntent(intent: WorkspaceIntent): WorkspaceCapability {\n  switch (intent.intent) {\n    case \"conversation.message.submit\":\n    case \"conversation.influence.select\":\n      return \"conversation\";\n    case \"frame-review.annotate\":\n    case \"frame.decision.accept\":\n    case \"frame.accept\":\n      return \"frame\";\n    case \"memory.proposal.review\":\n      return \"memory\";\n  }\n}\n\nfunction brokenQuery(\n  query: WorkspaceQuery,\n  message: string,\n  code:\n    | \"WORKSPACE_TRANSPORT_FAILED\"\n    | \"WORKSPACE_PROJECTION_INVALID\" = \"WORKSPACE_TRANSPORT_FAILED\",\n): WorkspaceQueryResult {\n  return decodeStrict(WorkspaceQueryResultSchema, {\n    status: \"broken\",\n    query,\n    diagnostic: { code, message },\n  });\n}\n\nfunction brokenIntent(capability: WorkspaceCapability, message: string): WorkspaceIntentResult {\n  return decodeStrict(WorkspaceIntentResultSchema, {\n    status: \"broken\",\n    capability,\n    diagnostic: { code: \"WORKSPACE_TRANSPORT_FAILED\", message },\n  });\n}\n\nfunction revisionKey(capability: WorkspaceCapability, scope: WorkspaceScope): string {\n  return scope.kind === \"project\"\n    ? `${capability}:project:${scope.projectId}`\n    : `${capability}:waypoint:${scope.projectId}:${scope.waypointId}`;\n}\n\nfunction queryRevisionKey(query: WorkspaceQuery): string {\n  switch (query.query) {\n    case \"conversation.read\":\n      return revisionKey(\"conversation\", query.scope);\n    case \"context-record.read\":\n      return revisionKey(\"conversation\", { kind: \"project\", projectId: query.projectId });\n    case \"frame-review.read\":\n      return revisionKey(\"frame\", { kind: \"project\", projectId: query.projectId });\n    case \"memory-library.read\":\n      return revisionKey(\"memory\", { kind: \"project\", projectId: query.projectId });\n  }\n}\n\nexport interface WorkspaceBridgeClient {\n  query(query: WorkspaceQuery): Promise<WorkspaceQueryResult>;\n  submit(intent: WorkspaceIntent): Promise<WorkspaceIntentResult>;\n  subscribe(listener: (notification: WorkspaceNotification) => void): () => void;\n  stop(): void;\n}\n\nconst stoppedText = \"Workspace bridge is stopped.\";\n\nconst closingDiagnostic = {\n  code: \"WORKSPACE_CAPABILITY_UNAVAILABLE\",\n  message: \"The workspace is unavailable while SlopStop closes.\",\n} as const;\n\n/** The query answer while SlopStop closes (D11): at the IPC gate and for work in flight at stop. */\nexport function workspaceQueryClosing(query: WorkspaceQuery): WorkspaceQueryResult {\n  return { status: \"unavailable\", query, diagnostic: closingDiagnostic };\n}\n\n/** The intent answer while SlopStop closes (D11). */\nexport function workspaceIntentClosing(capability: WorkspaceCapability): WorkspaceIntentResult {\n  return { status: \"unavailable\", capability, diagnostic: closingDiagnostic };\n}\n\nconst desktopText = {\n  \"invalid-command\": harnessSendFailureMessage({\n    ok: false,\n    error: { code: \"HARNESS_SESSION_MESSAGE_INVALID\" },\n  }),\n  ...bridgeNeutralText.desktop,\n  \"mismatched-event\": \"Harness returned a mismatched workspace response.\",\n} as const;\n\nconst protocolText = {\n  ...bridgeNeutralText.protocol,\n  uncorrelated: \"Harness returned an uncorrelated workspace response.\",\n  undecodable: \"Harness returned an invalid workspace response.\",\n} as const;\n\n/**\n * The fixed text a workspace request answers for each fault; `echo` names a mismatched echo. A\n * stopped request has no text: it answers the closing envelope.\n */\nfunction faultText(fault: Exclude<ProjectRequestFault, { kind: \"stopped\" }>, echo: string): string {\n  return Match.value(fault).pipe(\n    Match.discriminatorsExhaustive(\"kind\")({\n      transport: () => bridgeNeutralText.transport,\n      protocol: ({ reason }) => protocolText[reason],\n      harness: ({ message }) => message,\n      send: ({ code }) => harnessSendFailureMessage({ ok: false, error: { code } }),\n      unsent: () => stoppedText,\n      desktop: ({ reason }) => (reason === \"mismatched-echo\" ? echo : desktopText[reason]),\n    }),\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * The workspace bridge over a request core. Its notifications arrive through the core's observer\n * hook, so it opens no session subscription of its own. `afterStop` runs once, inside the\n * bridge's own stop: the standalone bridge ends its private core there.\n */\nexport function workspaceBridgeOver(\n  core: HarnessRequestCore,\n  clock: Clock,\n  afterStop: () => void,\n): WorkspaceBridgeClient {\n  const notificationListeners = new Set<(notification: WorkspaceNotification) => void>();\n  const projectionRevisions = new Map<string, number>();\n  let stopped = false;\n  const metadata = () => ({ messageId: clock.createId(), sentAt: clock.now() });\n\n  const publishNotification = (message: HarnessMessage) => {\n    if (message.event !== \"workspace.projection.invalidated\") return;\n    // An undecodable notification throws, after the session delivered it to every listener.\n    const parsed = decodeStrict(WorkspaceNotificationSchema, message.payload);\n    const key = revisionKey(parsed.capability, parsed.scope);\n    const previous = projectionRevisions.get(key);\n    if (previous !== undefined && parsed.revision <= previous) return;\n    projectionRevisions.set(key, parsed.revision);\n    for (const listener of notificationListeners) listener(parsed);\n  };\n  const removeObserver = core.observe(publishNotification);\n\n  /** A ready result older than the scope's floor is invalid; otherwise it raises the floor. */\n  const acceptQueryResult = (result: WorkspaceQueryResult): WorkspaceQueryResult => {\n    if (result.status !== \"ready\") return result;\n    const key = queryRevisionKey(result.query);\n    const previous = projectionRevisions.get(key);\n    if (previous !== undefined && result.projection.revision < previous) {\n      return brokenQuery(\n        result.query,\n        \"Harness returned an older workspace projection.\",\n        \"WORKSPACE_PROJECTION_INVALID\",\n      );\n    }\n    projectionRevisions.set(key, result.projection.revision);\n    return result;\n  };\n\n  /** One workspace request: a stopped bridge answers `unsent`; a reply that fails `check` is a mismatched echo. */\n  function request<Result>(\n    operation: Readonly<{\n      command: () => DesktopMessage;\n      event: HarnessMessage[\"event\"];\n      schema: Schema.Decoder<Result>;\n      check(result: Result): Result | undefined;\n      broken(fault: ProjectRequestFault): Result;\n    }>,\n  ): Promise<Result> {\n    if (stopped) return Promise.resolve(operation.broken({ kind: \"unsent\" }));\n    return answerRequest(\n      core.request(\"workspace\", operation.command, operation.event).pipe(\n        Effect.flatMap((value) => decodeReply(operation.schema, value)),\n        Effect.flatMap((result) => {\n          const accepted = operation.check(result);\n          return accepted === undefined\n            ? Effect.fail({ kind: \"desktop\", reason: \"mismatched-echo\" } as const)\n            : Effect.succeed(accepted);\n        }),\n      ),\n      operation.broken,\n    );\n  }\n\n  return {\n    query: (query) =>\n      request({\n        command: () => createWorkspaceQueryCommand(metadata(), query),\n        event: \"workspace.query.result\",\n        schema: WorkspaceQueryResultSchema,\n        check: (result) =>\n          isDeepStrictEqual(result.query, query) ? acceptQueryResult(result) : undefined,\n        broken: (fault) =>\n          fault.kind === \"stopped\"\n            ? workspaceQueryClosing(query)\n            : brokenQuery(\n                query,\n                faultText(fault, \"Harness returned a mismatched workspace query.\"),\n              ),\n      }),\n    submit: (intent) => {\n      const capability = capabilityForIntent(intent);\n      return request({\n        command: () => createWorkspaceIntentCommand(metadata(), intent),\n        event: \"workspace.intent.result\",\n        schema: WorkspaceIntentResultSchema,\n        check: (result) => (result.capability === capability ? result : undefined),\n        broken: (fault) =>\n          fault.kind === \"stopped\"\n            ? workspaceIntentClosing(capability)\n            : brokenIntent(\n                capability,\n                faultText(fault, \"Harness returned a mismatched workspace capability.\"),\n              ),\n      });\n    },\n    subscribe: (listener) => {\n      notificationListeners.add(listener);\n      return () => {\n        notificationListeners.delete(listener);\n      };\n    },\n    stop: () => {\n      if (stopped) return;\n      stopped = true;\n      // Settle first: an unsubscribe failure in `afterStop` surfaces only after.\n      core.failBridge(\"workspace\", { kind: \"stopped\" });\n      removeObserver();\n      notificationListeners.clear();\n      afterStop();\n    },\n  };\n}\n\n/** A standalone workspace bridge, with its own core over `session`. */\nexport function createWorkspaceBridge(options: WorkspaceBridgeOptions): WorkspaceBridgeClient {\n  const core = makeHarnessRequestCore(options.session);\n  return workspaceBridgeOver(core, options, core.unsubscribe);\n}\n"}},"schemaVersion":"1.0","thresholds":{"high":90,"low":80,"break":80},"testFiles":{"apps/desktop/src/main/harness-supervisor.test.ts":{"tests":[{"id":"0","name":"HarnessSupervisor
